HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000745/B004","root_001110/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_2a93fdf42387c9654e05","text":"1. **Comprehensive belonging establishes asymmetrical sufficiency.** **س م و B004** and **ء ر ض B001** span upper cover and lower ground; **غ ن ي B001–B002** is self-sufficiency and the capacity to suffice or replace. The fronted divine possession of the merism precedes emphatic self-sufficiency. I infer that the owner of the total field cannot depend on any item inside it, while every item can be supplied from that field. *Al-Ghanī* reads relationally as the asymmetry generated by comprehensive belonging.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B003","root_001110/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4ec66cb6c12698e37cd2","text":"2. **Praise does not wait for human acknowledgment.** **ح م د B003** is the one praiseworthy through many good traits and **B002** being found satisfactory. The previous ayah showed people stating the creator’s name without knowledge; here **ح م د** is predicated without a human praising verb. I infer that praiseworthiness is intrinsic and discoverable, not conferred. The pairing with **غ ن ي B001** makes independence from praise part of what warrants praise.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_adc433391c69cc07133b","text":"2. **The sufficiency that finite resources cannot measure.** Lexical evidence: `غ ن ي B002` is sufficing, availing, and replacing, while `ح م د B003` is the praiseworthy one marked by many good traits. Structural cue: totality in heavens and earth is followed by exclusive emphasis `هُوَ`; 31:27 will imagine all trees and serial seas exhausted while divine words remain. Assigned role: cosmic totality supplies the largest finite comparison, and self-sufficiency exceeds dependence on the whole comparison set. Abductive move (inferred): inexhaustibility of the words is the operational display of a source not depleted by what it owns or gives. Reading change: `ٱلْغَنِىُّ` reads as inexhaustible sufficiency sustaining total possession.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001110/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c6c648233ebb3b84d591","text":"1. **Ownership without acquisitive need.** Lexical evidence: `غ ن ي B001` is self-sufficiency and freedom from need, and `ح م د B001` is praise for praiseworthy action and benefaction. Structural cue: total possession is followed not by increase or extraction but by emphatic self-sufficiency; 31:31 will describe a ship moving by divine favor. Assigned role: possession establishes comprehensive authority, self-sufficiency removes need for what is possessed, and praise identifies beneficent rather than extractive exercise of authority. Abductive move (inferred): an owner who lacks need can order possessions for their benefit rather than to repair a deficiency in himself. Reading change: divine possession becomes compatible with giving because it is explicitly non-acquisitive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000355/B001","root_000355/B003","root_001110/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_cc54755fd2994f4b81cc","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:26:9\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\"]],[\"31:26:10\",\"root_000355\",[\"B001\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Her şeye sahip oluşun hemen ardından ihtiyaçsızlığın gelmesi, ilahi mülkiyeti eksikliği gidermek için toplayan değil, yarar sağlayabilen bir egemenlik olarak gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000025/B001","root_000745/B004","root_001110/B001","root_001110/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d3d259acc166d3b66135","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"31:26:4\",\"root_000745\",[\"B004\"]],[\"31:26:5\",\"root_000025\",[\"B001\"]],[\"31:26:9\",\"root_001110\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Üst ve alt alanların tümü Allah'a ait olduğundan, O bu alanın hiçbir parçasına bağımlı değildir; buna karşılık alanın her parçasını yeterli kılabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ee25985fed2841fd7918","text":"Ayah 31:27 immediately tested sufficiency against an imagined depletion apparatus: every tree-pen and serial sea fails before divine words do.
